Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The experiments variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 279 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 52 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by experiments generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 52. QED.
